## Leveling XP
Hunting XP can be gained though various means:
### Hunting and Fusion
Hunting experience can be gained by Hunting mobs with various methods and from [[Attribute Fusion]].
The amount of XP gained depends on the rarity of the shard.
|Shard Rarity|XP gained Hunting|XP gained Charming|XP gained Fusion|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|+75 Hunting XP|+750 Hunting XP|+18.75 Hunting XP|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+150 Hunting XP|+1,500 Hunting XP|+37.5 Hunting XP|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+300 Hunting XP|+3,000 Hunting XP|+75 Hunting XP|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+500 Hunting XP|+5,000 Hunting XP|+125 Hunting XP|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|+1,000 Hunting XP|+10,000 Hunting XP|+250 Hunting XP|
Hunting [[Glacite Walker|Glacite Walkers]] and [[Ghost|Ghosts]] grants 50% of the base Hunting XP.
Each dropped shard from Hunting or [[Charm Chance|Charming]] grants Hunting XP.
### Critter Safari
Catching critters with [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]] grants the same amount of Hunting XP as normal mob hunting, which can be increased depending on [[Safari Tickets|Safari Ticket]] tier via [[Hunting Wisdom]]. After catching certain amount of critters in a biome, player can claim [[Andrea|Safari Milestones]] to get more Hunting XP. [[Critterdex]] and [[Critterdex|Sparkling Critterdex]] also grant Hunting XP.
Every player also gets +5,000 Hunting XP per unique caught [[Critters|Critter]] on the [[Critter Safari]], maxing out at +185,000 Hunting XP per instance.
Critter Safari [[Floor Drops]] can grant 2,500-10,000 Hunting XP.
### Other
Players can also get Hunting experience from:
- [[David Hunterborough]]'s [[David Hunterborough|Trap Hunts]] and [[David Hunterborough|Salt Hunts]] milestones
- from [[Lushlilac|Lushlilac IV (4)]], [[Tender Wood|Tender Wood VIII (8)]] and [[Vinesap|Vinesap VIII (8)]] collection tiers;
- and from [[Trevor|Trapper Animals]].
### Increasing amount of Hunting XP gained
Hunting XP gain can be increased with [[Hunting Wisdom]].
Additionally, [[Critters]] grant more Hunting XP, depending on player's [[Bestiary]] tier for that mob.
## Hunting Methods
### Forest Hunting
[[Attributes|Forest Attributes]] require an [[Abysmal Lasso]] or better. To hunt with a Lasso, one must cast the [[Lassos|Lasso]] at them and wait for their stamina meter to deplete until REEL appears. Some mobs, like [[Dreadwing|Dreadwings]] and [[Cinderbat|Cinderbats]], have multiple stamina bars.
The [[Attributes|Hunter's Suppress]] attribute increases how much Lassos deplete a creature's stamina by.
### Water Hunting
[[Attributes|Water Attributes]] require a [[Basic Fishing Net]] or better. Some mobs are caught instantly, like [[Verdant|Verdants]] and [[Birries]], while others have health that is depleted upon being caught. The damage dealt by a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] is determined by your [[Pull]].
The [[Attributes|Eelastic]] attribute grants increased [[Pull]].
### Combat Hunting
[[Attributes|Combat Attributes]] require a [[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]] and [[Small Pocket Black Hole]] or better. Any melee [[Weapons|Weapon]] can be inserted into a [[Huntaxes|huntaxe]] to gain some of its stats. Damaging a mob with a [[Huntaxes|huntaxe]] deals damage to it until it reaches 1 [[Health]]. Once below 10% [[Health]], a Pocket Black Hole can be placed to absorb the mob and gain its [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].
The [[Attributes|Hunter's Fang]] attribute increases the stats gained from the weapons stored in your [[Huntaxes]]. The [[Attributes|Hunter's Pressure]] attribute increases the speed at which your [[Black Holes]] capture mobs at.
### Charm Hunting
Killing a [[Mob]] while any of the [[Charm Chance|Charming]] effects are active (like [[Salts|Salt]]) grants a small chance to capture their [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].
### Trap Hunting
Placing a [[Huntraps|Huntrap]] in a location where mobs have [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] will eventually capture a [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].
The [[Attributes|Forest Trap]] attribute increases the rate at which your [[Huntraps]] catch Forest Shards.
The [[Attributes|Combat Trap]] attribute increases the rate at which your [[Huntraps]] catch Combat Shards.

## Ways to boost XP gain
The amount of Mining XP the player receives can be increased through the [[Mining Wisdom]] stat. The maximum possible [[Mining Wisdom]] that can be achieved is displayed below.
- [[Candidate Jerry]] [ [[Cole]]]
- [[Mining Fiesta]]
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Silverfish Pet]] [[Lv100]]
- [[Divan's Drill]] [ [[Andesite Whetstone|Refined]] + [[Recombobulator 3000]] + [[Compact|Compact X]]]
- [[Booster Cookie]]
- [[Potions|Mining XP Boost III]]
- [[Heart of the Mountain|Seasoned Mineman 101]]
- [[Abicases|Actually Blue™ Abicase]]
## Mining Levels

### Item Abilities
Abilities of some items gain a bonus depending on player's Mining level
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
- [[Ammonite Pet]]'s Gift of the Ammonite grants 0.5 [[Fishing Speed]], 2 [[Speed]] and 2 [[Defense]] per Mining and [[Fishing]] level.
- [[Glacite Armor]]'s full set bonus — Expert Miner — grants 2 [[Mining Speed]] per Mining level.

# Blaze Slayer
Categories: Slayer, Blaze Slayer
Blaze Slayer is a [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] that involves killing [[Blaze|Blazes]] and at the end the [[Inferno Demonlord]]. A Tier III [[Voidgloom Seraph]] must be slain to unlock Blaze Slayer.
Boss: [[Inferno Demonlord]]
Main Crafting Resources: [[Derelict Ashe]] • [[Molten Powder]]
## Experience
The following [[Mob|Mobs]] grant experience towards completing an active Blaze Slayer quest. Some of the listed mobs appear at random only if the player has a higher tier slayer quest. While some mobs are much tougher, they provide much more experience.
|Location / Tier|Mobs|Base Combat XP ([[Combat]])|
|-|-|-|
|[[Khazad-dûm]]|[Lv15] [[Bald Blaze]]|10|
|[[Stronghold]]|[Lv70] [[Blaze]]|120|
|[[Stronghold]]|[Lv70] [[Mutated Blaze]]|120|
|[[Stronghold]]|[Lv80] [[Bezal]]|120|
|[[Smoldering Tomb]]|[Lv95] [[Smoldering Blaze]]|240|
|[[Smoldering Tomb]]|[Lv110] [[Millennia-Aged Blaze]]|660|
|[[Ruins of Ashfang]]|[Lv200] [[Ashfang]]|2000|
### Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.
|Mob|Stats|Spawned at/via|Mob Types|Item Drops|Non-Item Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv381] [[Flare Demon]]|[[Health]]: 8,750,000; [[Damage]]: 3,000|Tier III Blaze Slayer|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|1x [[Derelict Ashe]]|+2,857 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 1143 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv456] [[Kindleheart Demon]]|[[Health]]: 17,500,000; [[Damage]]: 6,000|Tier IV Blaze Slayer|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|2x [[Derelict Ashe]]|+3,420 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 1368 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv522] [[Burningsoul Demon]]|[[Health]]: 35,000,000; [[Damage]]: 9,000|Tier IV Blaze Slayer|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|5x [[Derelict Ashe]]|+3,915 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 1566 [[Experience]]|

Categories: Slayer, Enderman Slayer

Boss: [[Voidgloom Seraph]]

### Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.

- Vampire Slayer and [[Enderman Slayer]] are the only slayer quests exclusive to one island.
- Vampire Slayer is the first slayer to use a custom mob rather than vanilla minecraft mob types.
- Vampire Slayer is the only slayer that does not have minibosses.
- Vampire Slayer is the only slayer that does not say "More to come! More rewards will be added with new items in the future." in the Slayer LVL Rewards menu.
- Vampire Slayer is also the only slayer without a [[Mixins|Mixin]].

# Wolf Slayer
Categories: Slayer, Wolf Slayer
Wolf Slayer is a [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] that involves killing [[Wolf|wolves]] to spawn the [[Sven Packmaster]]. The player must first defeat the Tier II [[Tarantula Broodfather]] to unlock Wolf Slayer. See [[Slayer]] for specifics.
Boss: [[Sven Packmaster]]
Main Crafting Resources: [[Wolf Tooth]] • [[Golden Tooth]]
## Experience
The following [[Mob|Mobs]] give experience towards completing an active Wolf Slayer quest. Some of the listed mobs appear at random only if the player has a higher tier slayer quest. While some mobs are much tougher, they provide much more [[Experience]]. The amount of base [[Combat]] skill experience a mob gives is marked in the brackets.
|Location / Tier|Mobs|Base Combat XP ([[Combat]])|
|-|-|-|
|[[Ruins]]|[[Wolf]]|10|
|[[Ruins]]|[[Old Wolf]]|40|
|[[Howling Cave]]|[[Pack Spirit]]|15|
|[[Howling Cave]]|[[Howling Spirit]]|15|
|[[Howling Cave]]|[[Soul of the Alpha]]|50|
|[[Mithril Deposits]]|[[Belle]]|5|
### Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 12.5% (1 in 8) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.

|[Lv169] [[Sven Follower]]|[[Health]]: 120,000; [[Damage]]: 1,200|Tier IV Wolf Slayer|[[Animal]]|2x [[Wolf Tooth]]|+1,267 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 507 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv238] [[Sven Alpha]]|[[Health]]: 480,000; [[Damage]]: 2,200|Tier IV Wolf Slayer|[[Animal]]|5x [[Wolf Tooth]]|+1,785 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 714 [[Experience]]|

# Zombie Slayer
Aliases: Revenant Slayer, Revenant slayer
Categories: Slayer, Zombie Slayer
Zombie Slayer is a [[Slayer]] that involves killing [[Zombie|Zombies]] and at the end, the [[Revenant Horror]] or the [[Revenant Horror|Atoned Horror]]. The boss will always drop [[Revenant Flesh]].
Boss: [[Revenant Horror]]
Main Crafting Resources: [[Revenant Flesh]] • [[Revenant Viscera]]

# Floating Crystals
Aliases: Bat Crystal, Floating Crystal, Island Crystals, Roofed Forest Crystal
Categories: Spawnable islands, Crystal
Floating Crystals are entities that float in the air and provide an effect to the [[Private Island]].
With the exception of the [[Floating Crystals|Bat Crystal]], Floating Crystals can be right-clicked to pick them up and be placed elsewhere.
## Obtaining
Floating Crystals can either be [[Crafting|Crafted]] or come with a placed [[Locations|Island]]. Floating Crystals only appear if no others of the same type are on the [[Private Island]].
|Image|Crystal Name|Obtained via|Replaceable?|Radius|Effect|Recipe|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wheat Crystal]]|[[Wheat Crystal]]|[[Farming Island]] (Wheat VI (6) ([[Wheat]]))|✔|6|Places fully grown [[Wheat]] on any farmland below.; Performs an action every 5 seconds.||
|[[Desert Crystal]]|[[Desert Crystal]]|[[Desert Island]] (Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]]))|✔|16|Grows [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cactus]], and [[Dead Bush]] on any [[Sand]] below.; Performs an action every 30 seconds, caps at 7 plants grown.||
|[[Nether Wart Crystal]]|[[Nether Wart Crystal]]|[[Nether Wart Island]] (Netherrack I (1) ([[Netherrack]]))|✔|5|Places fully grown [[Nether Wart]] on any [[Soul Sand]] below.; Performs an action every 5 seconds.||
|[[Fishing Crystal]]|[[Fishing Crystal]]|[[Pond Island]] (Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]]))|✔|16|Allows the [[Player]] to catch [[Prismarine Crystals]] and Prismarine Shards ([[Prismarine Shard]]) on the [[Private Island]].||
|Bat Crystal|Bat Crystal|[[Roofed Forest Island]] (Dark Oak Log VI (6) ([[Dark Oak Log]]))|✕|20|Summons Bats ([[Bat (disambiguation)]]) and regenerates Dark Oak ([[Dark Oak Log]]) trees during the night.; If removed, making a new [[Roofed Forest Island]] generates a new Bat Crystal.||
|[[Winter Crystal]]|[[Winter Crystal]]|Winter Island ([[Winter Island (Item)]])|✔||Increases the speed of all Snow Minions ([[Snow Minion]]) and Ice Minions ([[Ice Minion]]) by 5%, and spawns a Gift ([[Gifts]]) ( White ([[White Gift]]), Green ([[Green Gift]]) or Red ([[Red Gift]])) once every SkyBlock Year ([[Time Systems]]) in a 15 block radius.||
|[[Farm Crystal]]|[[Farm Crystal]]|Crafting (Pumpkin VIII (8) ([[Pumpkin]]))|✔||Increases the speed of all [[Farming]] [[Minions]] by 10%.|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] x12 ×8 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] → [[Farm Crystal]]|
|[[Cornucopia Crystal]]|[[Cornucopia Crystal]]|Crafting|✔||Increases the speed of all [[Farming]] [[Minions]] by 10%.; Each unique [[Farming]] Minion ([[Minions]]) grants an additional 1% speed bonus.|Recipe: [[Cornucopia]] x8 ×8 + [[Farm Crystal]] → [[Cornucopia Crystal]]|
|[[Woodcutting Crystal]]|[[Woodcutting Crystal]]|Crafting (Spruce Log VII (7) ([[Spruce Log]]))|✔||Increases the speed of all [[Foraging]] [[Minions]] by 10%.|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spruce Log]] x12 ×8 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] → [[Woodcutting Crystal]]|
|[[Mithril Crystal]]|[[Mithril Crystal]]|Crafting (Mithril IV (4) ([[Mithril]]))|✔||Increases the speed of all [[Mining]] [[Minions]] by 10%.|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] → [[Mithril Crystal]]|
## Unobtainable floating crystals
Some Floating Crystals appear in the world and cannot be obtained by the player.
- [[Wheat Crystal]] - Found on the [[Farm]]. It acts the same as the one players can make.
- [[Carrot]]/ [[Potato]]/ [[Melon]]/ [[Pumpkin]] Crystal - Found on [[The Barn]].
- [[Flowers|Flower]] Crystal - Found outside the [[Flower House]].
## Tips
- If a [[Wheat Crystal]] is 2 blocks above the [[Dirt]], it can cover 6 blocks in each direction. A square can be made by filling in the corners.
- If a [[Desert Crystal]] is placed 5 blocks below the ending [[Sand]] block, it can cover 16 blocks in all directions to create a 33x33 square space of crop.

# Abiphone Contacts Trio
Categories: Special items, Items, Bits Shop
[Abiphone Contacts Trio]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Bits Shop]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 6,450 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TRIO_CONTACTS_ADDON
Tooltip: Consumable; Permanently adds +3 contact slots to your Abiphone plan, usable with any current and future Abiphones you own.; Up to 25 of these may be used.; Right-click to consume!; SPECIAL
Abiphone Contacts Trio is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item obtained from the [[Bits Shop]]. It grants an extra +3 contact slots to the [[Player]]'s [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] plan.
## Obtaining
Abiphone Contacts Trio can be obtained from the [[Bits Shop]] for 6,450 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Abiphone Contacts Trio grants an extra +3 contact slots for the [[Player]]'s [[Abiphones|Abiphone]]. This bonus stays even if the [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] model is upgraded, and the bonus ignores the [[Abiphones|Abiphone]]'s contact limit. Abiphone Contacts Trio can be used up to 25 times, which grants an additional +75 contact slots.
The Abiphone Contacts Trio can also be used to obtain [[Elizabeth]]'s [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|You've elevated your Abiphone contacts limit by +3!|
|Attempting to consume more than 25|You've reached the addons limit of 25!|
## Trivia
- Upon consuming an Abiphone Contacts Trio, the player is granted a levitation effect that lasts for a few seconds and stacks if multiple are used in a short time.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 13, 2022|AHN 0.16|Added Abiphone Contacts Trio.|
|Mar 23, 2026|Increased Abiphone Contacts Trio limit to 25.|

# Accessory Enrichment Swapper

Type: Item

Source: [[Bits Shop]]
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 200 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TALISMAN_ENRICHMENT_SWAPPER
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Swaps all enriched accessories in your accessory bag to another enrichment.; Right-click to open menu!; SPECIAL
The Accessory Enrichment Swapper is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] consumable Item that swaps all enriched accessories in the user's [[Accessory Bag]] to another [[Enrichments|enrichment]].
## Obtaining
The Accessory Enrichment Swapper can be bought for 200 [[Bits]] from the [[Bits Shop]], accessible at [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]].
## Usage
Right-clicking with an Accessory Enrichment Swapper will open the interface to choose the target enrichment. It will be consumed only after the enrichment is chosen.
[[Accessories]] in the user's inventory or in the hotbar won't receive the effect.
## Trivia

# Bachelor's Rose
Aliases: Bachelor, Bachelor Rose, Bachelors, Bachelors Rose, Bachelors rose
Categories: Special items, Items, Craftable items, Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, Wild Rose Collection, Collections
[Bachelor's Rose]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 512,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 204,800x (3,200 stacks (204,800 items total)) [[Wild Rose]] 64x Poppy ([[Flowers]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BACHELOR_ROSE
Tooltip: Can be gifted to any eligible NPC on a public island to invite them to visit your Garden.; Right-click on an NPC to use!; SPECIAL
A Bachelor's Rose is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wild Rose VII (7) ([[Wild Rose]])|8x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x Poppy ([[Flowers]])|Recipe: [[Compacted Wild Rose]] ×8 + [[Flowers]] x64 → Bachelor's Rose|
## Usage
A Bachelor's Rose can be given to any [[NPC]] that can visit the [[The Garden|Garden]] to invite them. Up to 5 Bachelor's Roses can be used per day. The same [[NPC]] also can't receive multiple Bachelor's Roses per day. This resets at midnight UTC.
A Bachelor's Rose cannot be used on MYTHIC rarity [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] or [[Beth]]. A Bachelor's Rose cannot be used on [[Mayor Election|ministers]].
Upon giving a rose to an NPC, the following message appears in chat:
[NPC] accepts your rose and is delighted to visit your Garden soon.
When attemping to give a rose to an ineligible NPC, the following message appears in chat:
Sorry, but this person is not eligible for a rose.
When attemping to give a rose to an NPC the player does not meet the requirements for, the following message appears in chat:
You do not meet the requirements to give a rose to this person.
## Trivia
- [[Gary]], [[Hendrik]], [[Sherry]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and [[St. Jerry]] can not be given a Bachelor's Rose during [[Jerry's Workshop]] if the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event is not active.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Bachelor's Rose.|
|Jan 26, 2026|Added Bachelor's Rose to [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 26, 2026|Fixed Bachelor's Rose not working on Mayors ([[Mayor Election]]).|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Bachelor's Rose as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Bachelor's Rose.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Added Bachelor's Rose to [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Fixed Bachelor's Rose not working on Mayors ([[Mayor Election]]).|
|Feb 2, 2026|Fixed Bachelor's Rose not working on [[Queen Nyx]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Bachelor's Rose as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Bat the Fish
Aliases: Bat The Fish
Categories: Special items, Items, The Fish
[Bat the Fish]
Type: Fish Family ([[The Fish Family]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Where is my cape!
Obtained: [[Bat Piñata]] drop
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BAT_THE_FISH
Tooltip: Where is my cape!; SPECIAL
Where is my cape!
Bat the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item that is a rare drop from naturally spawning [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]].
## Obtaining
Bat the Fish is obtained from [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]], with a chance to drop of 2%.
## Usage
Bat the Fish can be fed to [[Fann|Hootie]] to obtain [[Fann]]'s [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact. It can also be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- According to [[Fann]], Bat the Fish only swims at night.
## Tips
- Bat the Fish can be shown to [[Coral]] to meet the [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] collection requirement before being given to [[Fann|Hootie]] for [[Fann]]'s contact. This way, only one Bat the Fish is needed per player.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2019|0.7.3|Added Bat the Fish.|

Type: [[Collectable Items]]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BINGO_CARD
Tooltip: Obtained during the #th Bingo Event during [month] [year] for completing the entire Bingo Card!; Player: [player]; Playtime: [playtime]; Placement: [placement]; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL
Bingo Card is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Collectable Items|Collectable]] item. It is obtained by completing every [[Bingo|Goal]] on a [[Bingo]] [[Events|Event]]'s [[Bingo|Bingo Card]].
## Obtaining
### Bingo
A Bingo Card can be claimed from [[Jerry]] after completing all 20 [[Bingo|Goals]] on a [[Bingo]] [[Events|Event]]'s [[Bingo|Bingo Card]].
## Usage
A Bingo Card shows stats about which [[Bingo]] event's [[Bingo|Bingo Card]] was completed, which player completed them, and how long they took.
## Trivia
- The playtime indicated refers specifically to the time the player spent online on the bingo profile prior to completing the entire [[Bingo|Bingo Card]], not the time since the start of the Bingo event.
- Community goals do not affect obtaining the card, whether it be not having contributed in one or a goal not reaching its first tier.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 29, 2021|Added Bingo Card.|
|May 11, 2022|Added edition number in item name.|
|Nov 27, 2023|New Bingo Cards will have player placement on them.|

The Commemorative Mason Jar is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] trophy from the [[Harvest Feast]]. It is awarded to the player for reaching the final Feast Milestone for that event. In its tooltip, it features the notation of the event it was obtained in.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Commemorative Mason Jar.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Commemorative Mason Jar.|
|May 14, 2026|Changed Commemorative Mason Jar from [[Soulbound]] to Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
Linked from: [[Collectable Items]], [[Harvest Feast]]

# Critter Capsule
Aliases: Critter Capsules, Safari Capsule
Categories: Special items, Items, Confirmations needed, Galatea, Tools
[Critter Capsule]
Type: [[Capsule]]
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CRITTER_CAPSULE
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Capture RIGHT CLICK Throw your Critter Capsule in the direction you're facing. If it hits a  Critter, the capsule will attempt to catch them. Critters that drop a more valuable Shard are less likely to be caught.; This item will vanish from your inventory after leaving the Critter Safari!; SPECIAL CAPSULE
A Critter Capsule is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Capsule]]. It can only be interacted with in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Obtaining
Critter Capsules are given out by the [[Safari Manager]] upon entering the [[Critter Safari]]. The quantity of Critter Capsules given depend on the tier of Safari Ticket used.
- Selecting the Basic Safari Experience yields [[Critter Capsule]] 32 Critter Capsule.
- Selecting the Economy Safari Experience yields [[Critter Capsule]] 40 Critter Capsule.
- Selecting the Premium Safari Experience yields [[Critter Capsule]] 48 Critter Capsule.
- Selecting the First-Class Safari Experience yields [[Critter Capsule]] 56 Critter Capsule.
The player can also gain up to an additional [[Critter Capsule]] 8 Critter Capsules from upgrading the [[Archie|Critter Catcher]] perk.
## Usage
Critter Capsules can be thrown at any [[Critters|Critter]] in the [[Critter Safari]]. Upon hitting a Critter, it will bounce off and attempt to capture it, telegraphed by the Critter Capsule vibrating. Successfully capturing a Critter rewards their corresponding [[Attributes|Shard]] and consumes the Critter Capsule. When the player runs out of Critter Capsules, they are allowed to return to the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
The higher the rarity of the [[Attributes|Shard]] that the player's desired Critter drops, the rarer the chance of successfully capturing that Critter.
|Critter Rarity|Successful Catch Chance|Hard Pity|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|100% (1 in 1)|1|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|75% (1 in 1.33)|2|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|50% (1 in 2)|3|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25% (1 in 4)|5|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5% (1 in 20)|20|
If the player throws a Critter Capsule and it does not hit a Critter, it will drop as an item on the ground and can be picked up: after 5s[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], dropped Critter Capsules will automatically return to the inventory. Failing to capture a Critter will consume the Critter Capsule.
## Trivia
- The Critter Capsule resembles Pokéballs from Pokémon.
## Gallery
- A Critter Capsule while thrown
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Critter Capsule.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Increased the catch success rate for UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]), RARE ([[Rarity]]), and EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Critters]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added Critter Capsule.|

# Dead Bush of Love
Aliases: Dead Bush Of Love
Categories: Special items, Items, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Hypixel Gifts
[Dead Bush of Love]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: This item was given to the kind souls who helped so much testing SkyBlock! Much love ❤
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEAD_BUSH_OF_LOVE
Tooltip: This item was given to the kind souls who helped so much testing SkyBlock! Much love ; SPECIAL
The Dead Bush of Love is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item. Players (Staff + Hypixel Player Council) who helped test [[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]] before its public release received this item as a token of gratitude. Only a few players were given this opportunity, which is why this item is extremely rare.
## Gallery
- The old Dead Bush of Love tooltip
- The old tooltip when viewed through an NPC shop
## Trivia
- This item was last seen on the [[Auction House]] in January 2021.
  - The item was seen with a 1 billion coin bid, however it was later reported as a guild transfer, and the money and items were later returned.
  - The last time one was sold on the Auction House, it was a joke bid by xTear on MeltedMagma's auction and all money was later returned.
  - Currently, Doblox912 has two of them on his museum profile Pear.
- The name "Bush of Love" may come from the popular song "Bushes of Love" by "Bad Lip Reading" on YouTube.
- The last pink Dead Bush of Love before the [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] rarity color change was given to Alienator5000 (BernieSander previously) and was placed in an item frame on CookieNoRookie's island with the original pink color. However, it was later sold and turned into the recent red color.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Dead Bush of Love.|

Item ID: DIAMOND_NECRON_HEAD
Tooltip: Gear Score: 100 (110); Health: +50 (+55); Defense: +20 (+22); Strength: +10 (+11); Obtained from killing Bonzo in The Catacombs lots of times. Grants 2x stats on The Catacombs Floor I.; Involved in the dark arts due to his parents' insistence. Originally worked as a Circus clown.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Bonzo Collection 7.; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL DUNGEON HELMET
Diamond Heads are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Helmets]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
Each Diamond Head can be crafted from the [[Golden Heads|Golden Head]] of the same boss. The recipes are obtained from claiming the reward for defeating the corresponding [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Bosses|BOSS]] 1,000 times.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Diamond Bonzo Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|Bonzo VI (6) ([[Bonzo]])|1x Golden Bonzo Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
|Diamond Scarf Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|Scarf VI (6) ([[Scarf]])|1x Golden Scarf Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
|Diamond Professor Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|The Professor VI (6) ([[The Professor]])|1x Golden Professor Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
|Diamond Thorn Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|Thorn VI (6) ([[Thorn]])|1x Golden Thorn Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
|Diamond Livid Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|Livid VII (7) ([[Livid]])|1x Golden Livid Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
|Diamond Sadan Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|Sadan VII (7) ([[Sadan]])|1x Golden Sadan Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
|Diamond Necron Head ([[Diamond Heads]])|Necron VII (7) ([[Necron]])|1x Golden Necron Head ([[Golden Heads]]) 8x [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] ×8 + [[Golden Heads]] → [[Diamond Heads]]|
## Usage
Diamond Heads give [[Health]], [[Defense]] and [[Strength]], and also double the stats given by the helmet on their respective [[Dungeons|Dungeon Floors]].
## Essence Crafting
All Diamond Heads require the same amount of essence.
Essence Required; [[Diamond Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|50|50|
|✪✪|75|125|
|✪✪✪|125|250|
|✪✪✪✪|250; 10,000 [[Coins]]|500; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|500; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,000; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Knight Skin]]|[[Knight Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Taylor's Collection ([[Taylor]]) - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin will allow you to swap between 15 shiny versions!|
|[[Knight Skin]]|[[Knight Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Taylor's Collection ([[Taylor]]) - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Tips
- There is no reason to recombobulate Diamond Heads, as [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] rarity does not grant any extra reforge stat boosts over [[Rarity|SPECIAL]].
## Trivia
- Diamond Professor Head and Diamond Sadan Head used to have the same texture.
- Diamond Heads are one-time rewards. As they are from collection, they can only be claimed once per profile.
  - They can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 10,000,000 [[Coins]] if lost.

Diamond the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item that can be obtained through [[Odger]].
## Obtaining
Diamond the Fish is obtained by getting the [[Odger|Master Trophy Fisher]] title, which can be obtained by fishing up every [[Trophy Fish]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It can be claimed from [[Odger]] after reaching the requirements.
## Usage
Diamond the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- The lore of the item could be a reference to the lyrics of the song Diamonds by Rihanna.
  - The original lyrics are Shines bright like a diamond where diamond is replaced with fish.

# Dungeonbreaker
Aliases: DGB, Dungeon Breaker, Dungeonbreaker A, Dungeonbreaker B, Dungeonbreaker C, Dungeonbreaker D, Dungeonbreaker a
Categories: Special items, Items, Catacombs, Dungeons, Tools, Other Tools
[Dungeonbreaker]
Type: Dungeon Pickaxe
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Speed]]: 20
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Ophelia]]
Buy: 500x (7 stacks plus 52 (500 in total)) [[Wither Essence]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUNGEONBREAKER
Tooltip: Speed: +20; Ability: Dungeon Breaker DIG While in The Catacombs, consume 1⸕ charge to break a block. 20 blocks can be broken at a time, and re-appear after 10s. 2⸕ charges are regenerated each second.; Charges: 20\ 20⸕; SPECIAL DUNGEON PICKAXE
A Dungeonbreaker is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Dungeon [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]].
## Obtaining
A Dungeonbreaker is sold by [[Ophelia]] for 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Wither Essence]]. It requires [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] completion to purchase and use.
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Dungeonbreaker [[Speed]]: +20
[Effects]
[Ability: Dungeon Breaker DIG; While in The Catacombs (Catacombs), consume 1 charge to break a block. 20 blocks can be broken at a time, and re-appear after 10s. 2 charges are regenerated each second.; Charges: 20/20]
A Dungeonbreaker allows the player to temporarily break blocks in [[Dungeons]], costing 1 charge. Up to 20 blocks can be broken at once. 2 charges are restored per 1s. After 10s, broken blocks reappear. It cannot be used in puzzle rooms, on doors, or to pass through walls into a separate room.
## Trivia
- This item was created to replace stonking, a glitch that allowed players to walk through blocks by using a [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] enchanted with [[Efficiency|Efficiency X]].
- Since the introduction of this item, players normally have the Mining Fatigue effect while in Dungeons. This disappears while holding a Dungeonbreaker.
- While inside dungeons, the item gains invisible [[Efficiency|Efficiency X]] and Unbreaking X enchantments, as well as the minecraft:tool={default_mining_speed: 1024.0f} attribute.
- Despite being a pickaxe, this item is able to mine any block that is not part of a crypt inside the explored area of the dungeon, including trap rooms.
  - In most boss rooms, this item does not work. However, it works inside [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|the Wither Lords]] boss fight.
- A Dungeonbreaker can be used to mine in areas without Mining Fatigue, such as the [[Coal Mine]] and [[Jerry's Workshop]]. However, since it cannot have any upgrades applied to it, it is not an efficient mining tool.
- Although it cannot typically be used in puzzle rooms, the Dungeonbreaker can be used to cheese the secret in Tic Tac Toe.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 20, 2025|Added Dungeonbreaker.|
|Aug 28, 2025|Changed Dungeonbreaker from a stone pickaxe to a golden pickaxe.|
|Aug 28, 2025|Dungeonbreaker now removes Mining Fatigue while held.|
|Aug 28, 2025|Adjusted charge system from 30 maximum and 1/s to 20 maximum and 2/s.|
|Sep 4, 2025|Added Dungeonbreaker A, Dungeonbreaker B, Dungeonbreaker C, and Dungeonbreaker D as variants to test balancing.|
|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Finalized Dungeonbreaker A as Dungeonbreaker and removed other variants.|
|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Changed Dungeonbreaker from a golden pickaxe to a diamond pickaxe.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Added Dungeonbreaker.|

Extreme Bingo Card is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Collectable Items|Collectable]] item. It is obtained by completing every [[Bingo|Goal]] on an [[Bingo|Extreme Bingo]] [[Events|Event]]'s [[Bingo|Bingo Card]].
## Obtaining
### Extreme Bingo
A Extreme Bingo Card can be claimed from [[Jerry]] after completing all 20 [[Bingo|Goals]] on a [[Bingo|Extreme Bingo]] [[Events|Event]]'s [[Bingo|Bingo Card]].
## Usage
A Extreme Bingo Card shows stats about which [[Bingo|Extreme Bingo]] event's [[Bingo|Bingo Card]] was completed, which player completed them, and how long they took.
## Trivia
- The playtime indicated refers specifically to the time the player spent online on the bingo profile prior to completing the entire [[Bingo|Bingo Card]], not the time since the start of the Bingo event.
- Community goals do not affect obtaining the card, whether it be not having contributed in one or a goal not reaching its first tier.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

Fish the Fish has a 0.05% (1 in 2,000 rolls) chance to replace a non-sea creature catch in [[Torrhus Canyon]].

Gabagool the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item that can be obtained through [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]].
## Obtaining
Gabagool the Fish has a 0.0000254629% (1 in 3,927,273) chance to be generated per item harvested by an Inferno Minion using [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] (Hypergolic) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
## Usage
Gabagool the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GAME_BREAKER
Tooltip: This item was given to a player who reported a game breaking exploit.; What a guy!; To: Unknown From: [ዞ] The Hypixel Team For: Unknown; January 1970; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL
Game Breaker is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item rewarded to players who have reported a game-breaking exploit in Hypixel SkyBlock. Some of these reports include dupe glitches, server crashing methods, or skill abuse.
Take note that if you abuse a bug and then report it to the admins, you may not receive a Game Breaker and you may get banned!
## Obtaining
Game Breakers can sometimes be obtained by reporting a game-breaking exploit.
## Trivia
- In late May 2020, 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Jayavarmen announced that he would give a Game Breaker to the first person that reported a bug with the [[Wardrobe]] on the Alpha Hypixel Network.

# God Potion
Aliases: God, God Pot
Categories: Special items, Items, Bits Shop, Potions
[God Potion]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Buy: 1,500 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOD_POTION_2
Tooltip: Consume this potion to receive an assortment of positive potion effects!; Duration: 12h 0m; The duration of God Potions can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; SPECIAL
The God Potion is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] consumable item that gives the consumer a large assortment of positive potion effects and brews at max levels for a minimum of 12 hours.
## Obtaining
### NPC Vendors
God Potions can be bought for 1,500 [[Bits]] from the [[Bits Shop]], accessible at [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]].
## Usage
### God Potion Effect
The God Potion can be consumed via right-click to grant the following effects:
- [[Critical Potion|Critical IV]]
- [[Regeneration Potion|Regeneration IX]]
- [[Strength Potion|Strength VIII]]
- [[Agility Potion|Agility IV]]
- [[Night Vision Potion|Night Vision I]]
- [[Absorption Potion|Absorption VIII]]
- [[Burning Potion|Burning IV]]
- [[Stun Potion|Stun IV]]
- [[Dodge Potion|Dodge IV]]
- [[Experience Potion|Experience IV]]
- [[Mana Potion|Mana VIII]]
- [[Speed Potion|Speed VIII]]
- [[Water Breathing Potion|Water Breathing VI]]
- [[Potions|Alchemy XP Boost III]]
- [[Potions|Combat XP Boost III]]
- [[Potions|Enchanting XP Boost III]]
- [[Potions|Farming XP Boost III]]
- [[Potions|Fishing XP Boost III]]
- [[Potions|Foraging XP Boost III]]
- [[Potions|Mining XP Boost III]]
- [[Rabbit Potion|Rabbit VI]]
- [[Resistance Potion|Resistance VIII]]
- [[Archery Potion|Archery IV]]
- [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost IV]]
- [[Magic Find Potion|Magic Find IV]]
- [[Pet Luck Potion|Pet Luck IV]]
- [[Spirit Potion|Spirit IV]]
- [[Spelunker Potion|Spelunker V]]
- [[Adrenaline Potion|Adrenaline VIII]]
- [[Fire Resistance Potion|Fire Resistance I]]
- [[Haste Potion|Haste IV]]
- [[True Resistance Potion|True Resistance IV]]
- [[Jerry Candy]]
#### Duration
The default duration of the God Potion effect is 12 hours, this can be increased via the player's [[Alchemy]] skill levels up to 24 hours using the following formula: 12+0.24×(Player's Alchemy Level) The total duration can also be increased via [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Parrot Pet]] by 0.2%—20%. The maximum possible duration of the [[God Potion Effect]] from a single consumed God Potion is 28 hours and 48 minutes. Consuming God Potion while the effect is already active will sum up the durations up to 192 hours (8 days).
### Mixins
Mixins are used to upgrade God Potions to give them various buffs. There are currently 13 different [[Mixins]]. Some require reaching [[Slayer]] level 8 in their respective Slayer to be unlocked. They're obtained through various shops, such as [[Bartender]]'s or [[Agnes]]', or via crafting. Other Mixins are craftable and do not require Slayer requirements.
- The player cannot consume God Potions with [[Mixins]] for which they do not meet the [[Slayer]] requirement.
  - They however can use the same mixin to apply is to a God Potion inside a brewing stand, even without the [[Slayer]] requirement.
- Multiple [[Mixins]] can be put on a God Potion, and they will all be listed in the order in which they were applied.
### Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Consuming a God Potion (randomly chosen)|SIP! The God Potion grants you powers for ##h ##m!|
|Consuming a God Potion (randomly chosen)|GULP! The God Potion grants you powers for ##h ##m!|
|Consuming a God Potion (randomly chosen)|SLURP! The God Potion grants you powers for ##h ##m!|
|Attempting to consume a God Potion when doing so would exceed the limit of 192 hours|You can't handle that much Godly power at once!|
## Notes
- The duration of the [[God Potion Effect]] doesn't drop while the player is on any player's [[Private Island]], the effect will however remain. The duration is also paused inside the [[Catacombs]], but so will be the effect.
- God Potions bought before the [[City Projects/Bartender's Brewery|City Project/Bartender's Brewery]] have been turned into "[[God Potion (Legacy)]]", a now unobtainable form of the item, which still has the old effect.
- The [[God Potion Effect]] does not dissipate when dying.
- Using any regular potion (any potion that is not a God Potion) while under the effects of a God Potion will not increase the effect's timer, even if the God Potion has less time on it than the potion being used would give. This renders all potions included in a God Potion useless while it is active.
- Players can toggle off the effects granted by God Potion by navigating SkyBlock Menu → Booster Cookie → Toggle Potion Effects. This requires an active [[Booster Cookie]].
- If a player drinks a [[Milk Bucket]] or a [[Magical Milk Bucket]], all potion effects will be removed.
- The duration is not affected by any [[Potion Affinity Talisman|Potion Affinity Accessories]], but is still affected by the [[Parrot Pet|Parrot Pet's]] fourth ability, "Parrot Feather Infusion".
## Tips
- It is recommended for all players who can afford so to obtain and utilise the God Potion, as its effect is very strong and can help in all areas of SkyBlock. The easiest way to obtain this item is to claim the free [[Booster Cookie]], play until you've earned 1,500 [[Bits]], and then purchase it from [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]]. This method can only be used once however, as each player can only claim one free Booster Cookie. After that, a Booster Cookie may be purchased using [[SkyBlock Gems]] or from the [[Bazaar]].
## Trivia
- There used to be a bug where when a player was warped directly from one [[Dungeons]] server to another, there was a small chance that they will lose all buffs from a God Potion, despite the Tab list and SkyBlock Menu still showing that it is active. This has been patched since then.
- During the release of the [[Chili Pepper]] update, there was a bug causing God Potions to grant the [[Entropy Suppressor|Coldfused Reforge]] as a potion effect.
  - It lasted for the full duration of the God Potion. This means that even after it was patched, players could still keep the effect until the potion ran out.

Grants 2x stats on The Catacombs Floor I.; Involved in the dark arts due to his parents' insistence. Originally worked as a Circus clown.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor I Completion.; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL DUNGEON HELMET
Golden Heads are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Helmets]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
Golden Heads are obtained from claiming the reward for defeating the corresponding [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Bosses|BOSS]] 100 times.
If claimed inside a dungeon, they have a chance to have up to [[Star Upgrades|5 Stars]], [[Growth|Growth VI]], [[Protection|Protection VI]] and/or be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
### Shop Purchase
Golden Heads can also be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for 1 [[Gold Essence]] after unlocking them.
## Usage
Golden Heads give [[Health]], [[Defense]] and [[Strength]], and also double the stats given by the helmet on their respective [[Dungeons|Dungeon Floors]].
## Essence Crafting
All Golden Heads require the same amount of essence.
Essence Required; [[Gold Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|50|50|
|✪✪|75|125|
|✪✪✪|125|250|
|✪✪✪✪|250; 10,000 [[Coins]]|500; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|500; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,000; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- There is no reason to recombobulate Golden Heads, as [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] rarity does not grant any extra reforge stat boosts over [[Rarity|SPECIAL]].
## Trivia
- Golden Professor Head and Golden Sadan Head used to have the same texture.
  - Due to how similar their appearance is, a Golden Professor Head could easily be mistaken for a Golden Sadan Head.
  - The most noticeable difference is the darker eyebrows on the Golden Sadan Head.

- [[Diamond Heads]]

Two things are infinite, the universe and my ego.
Goldor the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] (7) [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Reward Chests]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Goldor the Fish has a chance to appear from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|6.4313% (1 in 15.5 runs)|6.0113% (1 in 16.6 runs)|61|10|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|3.4296% (1 in 29.2 runs)|5.989% (1 in 16.7 runs)|61|5|
## Usage
Goldor the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- Due to having low quality and low weight values, Goldor the Fish very rarely appears in the first slot of [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]], but much more frequently in later slots.
- It is one of three [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] items that are found in Catacombs [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] (7) [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Reward Chests]], along with [[Maxor the Fish]] and [[Storm the Fish]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HERRING_THE_FISH
Tooltip: Sometimes patience is the key to victory. Sometimes it leads to very little and it seems like it's not worth it. And you wonder why you waited so long for something so disappointing.; * Soulbound * SPECIAL
Herring the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained from the [[Kuudra Believer]]. Its NBT contains a tag named not_a_hint with the value f(x,y)=x^3+xy^2+y^2, u=(-125)i+(230)j.
## Obtaining
Herring the Fish is obtained from the Kuudra Believer after giving them 4,202,022 [[Coins]] while a [[Plhlegblast]] is within 3 blocks of the Kuudra Believer. [[Water Breathing Potion|Water Breathing]] and [[Fire Resistance Potion|Fire Resistance]] potions can help keep the Plhlegblast alive.
## Usage
Herring the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- The number refers to the date of the [[Crimson Isle]] release on the main server on April 20, 2022 or 4/20/2022
- The NBT tag "Not a hint" refers to how a Red Herring is intentionally misleading information, implying it has no relation to the hunt for [[Kuudra Follower Armor]].
- The tooltip of the item is a quote by Captain America in Spiderman: Homecoming.
- It was believed that the [[Wet Napkin]] requires owning Herring the Fish to obtain, however it was then proved wrong.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 16, 2022|Added Herring the Fish.|
|Jul 29, 2022|Herring the Fish was discovered.|

## Obtaining
An Icebreaker can be obtained as a [[Floor Drops|Floor Drop]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Usage
### Troodon Shard
An Icebreaker can be used to break ice at [[Coordinates|-104, 81, -95]], [[Coordinates|-109, 90, -27]], and [[Coordinates|-131, 79, -62]] in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. Breaking the ice frees a [[Troodon]] and allows it to be captured.
### Bell
An Icebreaker can also be used to break a frozen Bell at [[Coordinates|-68, 66, -43]], which is revealed by jumping on the iceberg it rests under until it flips.

# Kloonboat
Categories: Special items, Items
[Kloonboat]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])

[Item Properties]

Item ID: KLOONBOAT
Tooltip: Drop this item to decrease your FPS.; Immune to dropping.; To: Unknown From: Unknown; Edition #0; January 1970; SPECIAL
The Kloonboat is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item given to people by former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon.
## Information
The Kloonboat used to be able to be dropped, but this was patched when the [[Stash|Item Stash]] was introduced. The command that Minikloon uses to give this item to players is "/kloonboat <player>".
Alternate Kloonboat (no player info):
## Usage
The Kloonboat has a few, different effects it causes. All effects are client-side and cosmetic, and do not affect anyone, nor are seen by anyone, except the player holding the item.
- If you attempt to drop a Kloonboat, it will begin to rain.
- Each subsequent attempt to drop the Kloonboat, will result in the sky changing color, to the following:
  - First drop: Light Blue
  - Second drop: Dark Blue
  - Third drop: Yellow
  - Fourth drop: White
  - Fifth drop (or more): Nothing changes. The sky stays white, and it continues to rain. (until you switch servers)
## Given To
- Kloonboat #1: Minikloon stated his alternate account has this one
- Kloonboat #2 was obtained by SpeedyDrago, via an online raffle
- Kloonboat #6 was obtained by Mitrox, via raffle
- Kloonboat #9 was obtained by luvluvluv777, via unknown means
- Kloonboat #10 was obtained by Kezzbo, via raffle
- Kloonboat #11 was obtained by SpudManTaken_, given on accident (was supposed to be an [[Ancient Elevator]])
- Kloonboat #12 was obtained by mallo_YT, via unknown means
- Kloonboat #13 was obtained by RMF_playz, by Minikloon himself
- Kloonboat #15 was obtained by OuterSnas, by Minikloon himself
- Kloonboat #16 was obtained by TheOriginalAce, by Minikloon and displayed on BenC1ark's island
- Kloonboat #N/A was obtained by hugoland, via unknown means (per comment on wiki)
- Kloonboat #N/A was obtained by TimeDeo, via unknown means (per comment on wiki)
The rest of the "Kloonboats" are currently unaccounted for.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Kloonboat.|

# Kuudra Follower Armor
Aliases: Kuudra Armor, Kuudra Follower Boots, Kuudra Follower Chestplate, Kuudra Follower Helmet, Kuudra Follower Leggings, Kuudra armor
Categories: Special items, Armor, Armor Sets
[Kuudra Follower Armor]
Type: [[Armor]] Set
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No

Color: ⁠ #35530A
[Item Metadata]
Helmet Item ID: KUUDRA_FOLLOWER_HELMET
Chestplate Item ID: KUUDRA_FOLLOWER_CHESTPLATE
Leggings Item ID: KUUDRA_FOLLOWER_LEGGINGS
Boots Item ID: KUUDRA_FOLLOWER_BOOTS
Tooltip: Full Set Bonus: Follower Shows that you are a true follower of Kuudra.; Hunter: [player]; Edition # #month #day, #year; This item can be reforged!; * Soulbound * SPECIAL HELMET
Kuudra Follower Armor is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Armor]] set.
## Obtaining
### Helmet
To obtain the Kuudra Follower Helmet, the player must have obtained the [[Wet Napkin]] on their own, which can be fished up by following these steps:
- Donate at least 1,000,000 [[Coins]] to the [[Kuudra Believer]] and complete a [[Kuudra]] boss fight within the next 30 minutes. The [[Weird Sailor]] will then appear at [[Coordinates|-131, 93, -177]] in the [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
- Complete the [[Weird Sailor]]'s dialogue.
- Use a lava fishing rod near the [[Weird Sailor]] with [[Ice Bait]], while wearing a full [[Elegant Tuxedo]] and a [[Bonzo's Mask]] with [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration I]].
In addition to the prerequisite above, a [[Plhlegblast]] must be within 3 blocks of the [[Kuudra Believer]]. Once all these requirements are met, the player can claim the Kuudra Follower Helmet by donating the number of [[Coins]] given by the formula below to the [[Kuudra Believer]]. The number of [[Coins]] is always in the range of 13,331,070–13,599,850 [[Coins]]. Number of Coins=13331996+2⋅(Day of the Year in EST)2−58⋅(Player Name Length) Day of the Year in EST is the number of days since the last New Year's Eve, in the UTC−05:00 time zone (Eastern Standard Time, with no change for daylight saving time). For example, it would be 8 on January 8, or 44 on February 13. For October 27, 2026, the number is 300. Player Name Length is the number of characters in the player's Minecraft player name.
#### Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Kuudra Follower Helmet
 calcname  = Calculate Donation Cost
 template  = Template:Calculator/Kuudra Follower Helmet
 form      = kuudraFollowerHelmetCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = playerName|Player Username|user01|string
 param     = dateOpt|Date Used|true|select|Current Date,Custom EST Date|Custom EST Date=year,month,day
 param     = year|Year|2019|int|0-99999
 param     = month|Month|6|int|1-12
 param     = day|Day|12|int|1-31
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
Show/Hide Explanation of Formula
- The hint was from the tooltip of [[Wet Napkin]], which reads: "The total -------- plus time times time, all that subtracted from thrice the commencement, then add -- of yourself, the whole thing elbuod."
- The total -------- means the total number of Netherrack blocks in the [[Crimson Isle]], which is 21408112.
- time times time means to multiply the current day of the year by itself. This uses Eastern Standard Time, the time zone of Hypixel's server.
- thrice the commencement means three times the thread ID of the Crimson Isle release patch notes (https://hypixel.net/threads/4914038/), which is 4914038. Three times this is 14742114.
- -- of yourself means 29 times the number of characters in the Minecraft player name of the player claiming the helmet. The 29 comes from the number of hidden Among Us characters scattered throughout the map art of the [[Wet Napkin]].
- the whole thing elbuod means to multiply the result by -2, since elbuod is the word double backwards.
- Combining all of this, we get −2⋅(3⋅(4914038)−(21408112+(Day of the Year in EST)2)+29⋅(Player Name Length)).
- This simplifies to 13331996+2⋅(Day of the Year in EST)2−58⋅(Player Name Length).
### Chestplate
To obtain the Kuudra Follower Chestplate, at least 6 players are needed. These 6 players must be wearing [[Ember Armor]] or [[Rekindled Ember Armor]] and have a level 81 or higher [[Kuudra Pet]] summoned. They must stand in pairs in the following locations.
- 2 in the [[Stronghold]].
- 2 in the [[Dragontail Townsquare]].
- 2 in the [[Scarleton Plaza]].
The two players standing in the [[Stronghold]], as well as all other players on the server, can then speak to [[Arch]] to claim the chestplate.
### Leggings
The Kuudra Follower Leggings can be obtained by following these steps:
- Click the bookshelf at [[Coordinates|10, 127, -938]] in the [[Cathedral]] while having at least 4000 [[Intelligence]]. The following chat message will appear: You use your mana to pull a book out. Most of it is unreadable but you do notice a 2 and a 22...
In addition to the prerequisite above, click the block at [[Coordinates|-373, 82, -880]] inside the [[Blazing Volcano]] while holding a [[Drills|Drill]] during a [[Blazing Volcano|CATACLYSMIC]] volcano eruption. A menu will open, allowing the player to claim the [[Kuudra Relic]]. Show the [[Kuudra Relic]] to the [[Kuudra Archeologist]] to claim the Kuudra Follower Leggings.
### Boots
The Kuudra Follower Boots can be obtained by following these steps:
- Type one of the following lines in chat near the [[Kuudra Loremaster]] in the [[Scarleton]]:
  - THE BRIDGE IS COLLAPSING, GET TO THE CENTER!
  - Those orbs strengthen Kuudra! Shoot them down with your cannons!
  - A Dropship! Take it down before it's too late!
In addition to the prerequisite above, travel to the [[Deep Caverns]] on Tuesdays and cross the void to the old [[Blazing Fortress]]. The [[Kuudra Loremaster]] will appear at [[Coordinates|-256.5, 146, 120.5]]. The player will be able to claim the Kuudra Follower Boots if the following requirements are met:
- The player must have 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] and 1,024 [[Enchanted Netherrack]] in their inventory.
- The player must have at least 1,000,000 collection in both [[Netherrack]] and [[Magma Cream]].
## Usage
Wearing the full set of Kuudra Follower Armor during a [[Blazing Volcano|CATACLYSMIC]] eruption allows the player to purchase the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] from [[Drakuu]].
Additionally, wearing the full set while holding a [[Golden Ball]] allows the player to exchange it with [[Drakuu]] for a [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque|Exquisite Kuudra Teeth Plaque]].
## Tips
- [[Plhlegblast]] quickly dies in lava since it is not immune to lava damage. A [[Lead]] can be used to move it.
- [[Plhlegblast]] takes suffocation damage when outside of water. A [[Water Breathing Potion|Water Breathing VI]] Splash Potion can be used to keep it alive in the air, as it completely prevents suffocation damage.
- The current faction of a helper for the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Chestplate]] is irrelevant.
- Wearing [[Bat Person Armor]] with [[Reforging|Necrotic reforge]] during [[Spooky Festival]] is a budget way to effectively raise [[Intelligence]]. The Sighted accessory power can also help reach the high intelligence requirement.
- Using [[Bat Person Talisman]]'s Ability combining with [[Spirit Sceptre]] can raise [[Intelligence]] a little for several seconds, useful for when [[Armor]], [[Equipment]], and [[Accessory Power]] are still incapable to reach 4000 [[Intelligence]].
- Contributions from [[Co-op]] members count toward the collection requirements for the boots.
## Trivia
- Kuudra Follower Armor is the only obtainable [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] armor set.
- Of all pieces, the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Chestplate]] is the easiest to obtain - the player to claim the piece doesn't require anything other than talking to Arch as long as there are 6 players helping, while other pieces require the player completing certain tasks on their own.
- The lines for obtaining Kuudra Follower Boots were removed as it was the lines before the Kuudra fight revamp.

# Kuudra Relic
Categories: Special items, Items, Crimson Isle
[Kuudra Relic]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: Right-Click a specific block with any Drill.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_RELIC
Tooltip: Quest item; * Soulbound * SPECIAL
The Kuudra Relic is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Quest Item for obtaining [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Leggings]].
## Obtaining
The Kuudra Relic can be obtained following the steps:
- Click the bookshelf at [[Coordinates|10, 127, -938]] on [[Crimson Isle]] with over 4000 [[Intelligence]]. Doing so will give the following message: You use your mana to pull a book out. Most of it is unreadable but you do notice a 2 and a 22...
- Right-Clicking [[Coordinates|-373, 82, -880]] inside [[Blazing Volcano]] on [[Crimson Isle]] with any [[Drills|Drill]] during a [[Blazing Volcano|CATACLYSMIC]] volcanic eruption, a menu opens for claiming the Kuudra Relic. The player must reach the [[Heart of the Mountain]] level requirement of the drill.
## Usage
The Kuudra Relic can be brought to [[Kuudra Archeologist]] to obtain [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Leggings]].
## Trivia
- [[Coordinates|-373, 82, -880]] is obtained from the formula ∫2x−2x5+7x3dx [[Kuudra Archeologist]] has given with "2" and "22" input into bounds (∫2222x−2x5+7x3dx).
- Despite the name containing "Relic", which is usually used by 4th tier [[Accessories]] (For example [[Titanium Relic]], [[Candy Relic]]), it is not an accessory.
- This item uses the same texture as [[Fire Talisman]].
- This item will not be consumed upon claiming the armor piece.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Kuudra Relic.|
|Jan 11, 2023|0.17.2|Changed the requirement for the Kuudra Relic from [[Divan's Drill]] to any Drill ([[Drills]]).|

A Lush Lily Pad is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Quests|Quest Item]] found in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Obtaining
Lush Lily Pads can be harvested by the water pond in the Cavern biome in the [[Critter Safari]]. This pond can be found by climbing up the ladder near the Cavern biome's entrance, and is located at [[Coordinates|-110, 81, 38]].
## Usage
Lush Lily Pads can be fed to a [[Scrappy]] to get it to come out of its shell, allowing the player to capture it and obtain its loot.

# Masterful Critter Capsule
Categories: Special items, Items, Galatea, Tools
[Masterful Critter Capsule]

Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])

Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Certain Capture RIGHT CLICK Throw your Masterful Critter Capsule in the direction you're facing. If it hits a  Critter, it will catch them.; This item will vanish from your inventory after leaving the Critter Safari!; SPECIAL CAPSULE
A Masterful Critter Capsule is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Capsule]]. It can only be interacted with in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Obtaining
The player will spawn with [[Masterful Critter Capsule]] 1 Masterful Critter Capsule upon entering the [[Critter Safari]] when purchasing the [[Archie|Critter Master]] perk from [[Archie]].
## Usage
A Masterful Critter Capsule can be thrown at any [[Critters|Critter]] in the [[Critter Safari]]. Upon hitting a Critter, it will capture the Critter with a 100% success rate.
If the player throws a Masterful Critter Capsule and it does not hit a Critter, it will drop as an item on the ground and can be picked up.
## Tip
- It is recommended to use Masterful Critter Capsules at [[Doomspiral]] or [[Wumpa]], due to its long boss fight and intense difficulty or [[Macaw|Macaws]] due to also only having 5% chance.
## Trivia
- The Masterful Critter Capsule references the Master Ball from the Pokémon franchise.
## Gallery

Item ID: MATRIARCH_PARFUM
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume to apply a pungent smell upon yourself, confusing The Matriarch in the Crimson Isle into releasing new pearls.; Can be used up to 4 times a day!; Right-click to consume!; SPECIAL
Matriarch's Perfume is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item that resets [[The Matriarch]]'s cooldown.
## Obtaining
Matriarch's Perfume can be obtained from the [[Bits Shop]] for 1,200 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Once 3 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] have been collected from [[The Matriarch]], Matriarch's Perfume can be consumed to make it produce 3 more. This can be done up to 4 times a day, meaning a total of 15 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] can be obtained per day, or a total of 18 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] if the [[Attributes|Matriarch Cubs]] attribute procs every time.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Already consumed 4|You've already used perfume 4 times in the last 24 hours! You have to wait, otherwise the smell might stick to you forever!|
|Uncollected Heavy Pearls ([[Heavy Pearl]]) left|There are pearls left in the matriarch! Applying the perfume like this could confuse the beast and cause her to implode.|
## Tips
- When the pearls are sold on [[Bazaar]], this effectively grants per [[Bits|Bit]] spent or per [[Bits|Bit]] spent with [[Attributes|Matriarch Cubs X]], which is ineffective, making this item only viable for [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players.

## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Maxor the Fish has a chance to appear from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|6.4313% (1 in 15.5 runs)|6.0113% (1 in 16.6 runs)|61|10|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|3.4296% (1 in 29.2 runs)|5.989% (1 in 16.7 runs)|61|5|
## Usage
Maxor the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- Due to having low quality and low weight values, Maxor the Fish very rarely appears in the first slot of [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]], but much more frequently in later slots.
- It is one of three [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] items that are found in Catacombs [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] (7) [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Reward Chests]], along with [[Goldor the Fish]] and [[Storm the Fish]].

# Mementos
Aliases: Memento
Categories: Special items
[Mementos]
Mementos are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items that commemorate the player's participation in events and other occasions in [[SkyBlock]].
## Mechanics
Most of the Mementos display the acquirer's name, edition number and the date it was obtained on in its lore.
Most of the time, when traded or auctioned, the acquirer's name will be hidden, this is not the case for [[Basket of Hope from the Great Potato War]]. The edition number and date received will be retained.
Every Memento item can be added to the [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Museum]] category.

Categories: Special items, Items, Tools, Mining
[Metal Detector]
Type: Quest Item

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DWARVEN_METAL_DETECTOR
Tooltip: Used to find lost Dwarven treasures in the Mines of Divan. The Metal Detector beeps louder and faster the closer you are to a buried treasure.; SPECIAL
A Metal Detector is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item that allows the player to find treasures buried in the gold of the [[Mines of Divan]].
A beeping sound will play when the Metal Detector is held. The closer the player is to treasure, the faster and higher pitched the beeps will be. Additionally, in the player's action bar, TREASURE will be displayed along with a meters counter, which shows how far away the player is from treasure.

# Mob the Fish
Aliases: Mob The Fish
Categories: Special items, Items, The Fish
Am I a parasite?
[Mob the Fish]
Type: Fish Family ([[The Fish Family]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Am I a parasite?
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOB_THE_FISH
Tooltip: Am I a parasite?; SPECIAL
Mob the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained as a rare drop from killing any mob on [[Galatea]].
## Obtaining
Mob the Fish has a 0.05% (1 in 2,000) chance to be obtained from killing any mob on [[Moonglade Marsh]] or [[Torrhus Canyon]].
Every 2,500th mob kill on [[Moonglade Marsh]] is guaranteed to drop Mob the Fish. The player's progress towards this can be viewed using the [[Pity and RNG Meters|/pity]] command.
## Usage
Mob the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Mob the Fish.|
|Mar 23, 2026|Mob the Fish can now be sold to NPCs for 50,000 [[Coins]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Mob the Fish.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Mob the Fish can now be sold to NPCs for 50,000 [[Coins]].|

# New Year Cake
Aliases: New Year's Cake
Categories: Special items, Items, Events, New Year Celebration, Cake
[New Year Cake]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Baker]]
Uses: Filling the Cake Bag ([[New Year Cake Bag]]) for additional [[Health]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NEW_YEAR_CAKE
Tooltip: Given to every player as a celebration for the [year] SkyBlock year!; SPECIAL
At the end of each year I bake cakes for everyone in town to celebrate the year. I made one especially for you, here you go.
New Year Cake is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item players can obtain from the [[Baker]] every SkyBlock Year during the [[New Year Celebration]] (29-31 of Late Winter).
Each cake can only be claimed once per player, and is unique to the SkyBlock year it was received. This means cakes from previous years are no longer obtainable. The only way to get them is buying them from another player or the [[Auction House]].
This results in recent New Year Cakes fetching a low value in the Auction House, while certain cakes, mainly the first ten, year 69 and year 100 ([[Century Cakes|Century Cake]]) are worth millions of coins each.
## Usage
New Year Cakes can be stored in the [[New Year Cake Bag]]. Each cake from a SkyBlock year adds +1 [[Health]] to the [[New Year Cake Bag]] while stored inside.
## Trivia
- Players were able to get the 0th New Year Cake in Year 71, but the item was subsequently blacklisted. Some players, however, received the 71st Cake as expected, and some received the 72nd Cake in error.
- A celebration happens the last three days of each SkyBlock year, and the New Year Cake received shows the year that is ending instead of the next year. For example, at the end of SkyBlock year 1, right before the start of year 2, the cake showed the 1st SkyBlock year.
- There are currently 521? (Early Autumn 29th, 522, 07:44 PM | Click to Purge the Cache) unique cakes, as there have been 521? [[New Year Celebration|New Year Celebrations]] so far.
- Players were able to get multiple cakes in Year 1 by switching hub servers. This was patched for Year 3.
- Because of a glitch in obtaining the cake, Year 2 cakes are the most expensive cakes.
- During SkyBlock Year 144-146 (from June 23 to July 4, 2021), the [[Baking Specialist]] appeared at [[Coordinates|-12, 70, -44]] and handed out the Year 143 New Year Cake, because Hypixel was in maintenance mode during the [[New Year Celebration]] of Year 143. During the New Year Celebration of Year 144 and 145, both the Baker and Baking Specialist appeared.
- There was a bug where if you keep the New Year Cake claim menu open into the next year, you could claim the next New Year Cake (ex: if the New Year Cake is Year 100, you could wait in the menu until the event is over to claim a Year 101) This has since been patched.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added New Year Cake.|
|May 11, 2022|Added edition number in item name.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed being able to claim next year's New Year Cake if you keep the menu open after the [[Baker]] disappears.|

[Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Bits Shop]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 8,000 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POCKET_SACK_IN_A_SACK
Tooltip: Combine this item in an anvil with any sack to increase its capacity by +200% its original capacity.; This item can be combined with a sack up to three times!; It's a pocket sack, to be stitched inside another sack!; SPECIAL
Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item obtained from the [[Bits Shop]]. It triples a [[Sacks|Sack's]] capacity when combined in an [[Anvil]].
## Obtaining
Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack can be obtained from the [[Bits Shop]] for 8,000 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack can be combined with any [[Sacks|Sack]] in an [[Anvil]] to increase the Sack's capacity by 200% of its original capacity. Up to three can be applied per Sack.
Assuming a large sack (holding 20,160 items) as base:
|Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack used|Upgraded sack size|Equivalent number of unupgraded sacks|
|-|-|-|
|0|20,160|1|
|1|60,480|3|
|2|100,800|5|
|3|141,120|7|
Upgrading a [[Sacks|Sack]] with a Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack also upgrades its [[Rarity]]. This only happens once, it does not stack with multiple Pocket Sack-in-a-Sacks.
## Tips
- It is generally cheaper to get multiple [[Sacks]] to combine their capacity rather than upgrading one with a Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack. However, using this item will leave more space in your [[Sack of Sacks]].
  - Some [[Sacks]] cannot combine their capacity (see [[Sacks|Sacks#Bugs]])
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 13, 2022|AHN 0.16|Added Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack.|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Allow Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack to be used up to 3 times on the same Sack.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Balanced Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack storage size calculation.|
Linked from: [[Bits Shop]], [[Sacks]], [[Special Items]]

# Portalizer
Categories: Special items, Items, Bits Shop
[Portalizer]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Bits Shop]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 4,800 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PORTALIZER
Tooltip: Combine in an anvil with any Travel Scroll to transform it into a Portal item which can be placed on your private island.; Note: Some portals are available in various collections with cheap recipes!; SPECIAL
Portalizer is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item obtained from the [[Bits Shop]]. It turns a [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]] into a [[Portals|Portal]] when combined in an [[Anvil]].
## Obtaining
Portalizer can be obtained from the [[Bits Shop]] for 4,800 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Portalizer can be combined with any [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]] in an [[Anvil]] to turn it into a [[Portals]]. This includes [[Travel Scrolls]] that don't have an ordinary [[Portals|Portal]] counterpart.
## Trivia
- This item should only be used on Travel Scrolls leading to secondary locations, as the other portals are far less expensive to obtain.
- When the portal to a location has not yet been created, the anvil will display an error, and its lore will display the following text: Error! This travel scroll doesn't have a portal item. Ask an admin to add one immediately!
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 13, 2022|AHN 0.16|Added Portalizer.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added Portalizer.|
