# Priceless the Fish
Aliases: Priceless The Fish
Categories: Special items, Items, The Fish
[Priceless the Fish]
Type: Fish Family ([[The Fish Family]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PRICELESS_THE_FISH
Tooltip: The living proof that fish understand very little about economics.; SPECIAL
Priceless the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained from [[Shen's Auction]]. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
Priceless the Fish can sometimes appear on [[Shen's Auction]], and can be bid on by players. The top 5 bidders receive the item when the auction ends.
## Usage
Priceless the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## History

## Obtaining
A Purple Gem can be obtained as a [[Floor Drops|Floor Drop]] in the Canyon Biome in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Usage
### Gemzie Shard
A Purple Gem can be placed in the pedestal marked by a Pearlescent Froglight in the Canyon Biome. Placing a [[Orange Gem]] and [[Lime Gem]] will open the gates, allowing the player to capture 3 [[Gemzie|Gemzies]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RABBIT_THE_FISH
Tooltip: Mmm, oh I lom chomlate so mumch!; I...cam't get enoumgh of it! It's the bemst, the absolutme bemst!; My...chomlate...; SPECIAL
Rabbit the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] used to purchase [[Fish Chocolat à la Vapeur]] from the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Obtaining
Rabbit the Fish can rarely be found when opening [[Chocolate Eggs]]. It sometimes appears in a random slot of menu when the rabbit lottery is rolling.
## Usage
Rabbit the Fish can be used to purchase [[Fish Chocolat à la Vapeur]] from the [[Chocolate Factory]]. It can also be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Tips
- If you intend to obtain a Rabbit the Fish, do not instantly close the menu after finding an egg. If you close the menu without clicking on the trophy, it will be lost.
## History

Remote Perk Shop is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item purchasable from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Perk Shop]] for 100,000 [[Coins]]. As the name suggests, it allows the player to access the Perk Shop remotely. It stays with the player after the Kuudra run is over.
## Trivia
- You must have the coins in your bank to purchase this item. It will not take the coins from your purse.
  - As the Personal Bank is disabled inside Kuudra's Hollow, if you didn't have enough coins in your bank, you have to leave and rejoin to purchase the item.
- When bought it is renamed to [[Rarity|Open Shop]]

Rift Timecharms are a group of [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items obtainable in the [[Rift Dimension]].
There are 8 Timecharms, which can be secured in the player's [[Rift Gallery]] to grant access to a wider variety of [[Locations|Zones]], granting the player [[SkyBlock Levels|100 SkyBlock XP]] per Timecharm and increasing the stats on the [[Rift Necklace]].
## Rift Timecharms
|Slot|Name|Access|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Supreme Timecharm]]|[[Supreme Timecharm]]|[[West Village]]; [[Dreadfarm]]; [[Mirrorverse]]; [[Cake House]]|Recipe: [[Bottled Odonata]] ×4 + [[Lil' Pad]] x16 ×2 + [[Deadgehog Spine]] x12 ×2 + [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] x4 → [[Supreme Timecharm]]|
|[[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]]|[[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]]|[[Village Plaza]]|Recipe: [[Caducous Stem Bunch]] + [[Metaphoric Egg]] x2 ×4 + [[Agaricus Cap Bunch]] + [[Proto-Chicken]] + [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] ×2 → [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]]|
|[[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]]|[[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]]|[[Village Plaza]]|[[Dr. Emmett]] 10x [[Shadow Crux]] 10,000 [[Motes]]|
|[[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]]|[[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]]|[[Living Cave]]|Barry (Rift) ([[Barry]]) 16x [[Scribe Crux]] [[Barry's Montgray Pen]]|
|[[Living Timecharm]]|[[Living Timecharm]]|[[Colosseum]]|Recipe: [[Living Metal]] x20 ×8 + [[Frosty Crux]] x20 → [[Living Timecharm]]|
|[[Globulate Timecharm]]|[[Globulate Timecharm]]|[[Barrier Street]]; [[Stillgore Château]]|Recipe: [[Bacte Fragment]] ×8 + [[Shy Crux]] x20 → [[Globulate Timecharm]]|
|[[Vampiric Timecharm]]|[[Vampiric Timecharm]]|[[The Mountaintop]]|Recipe: [[Bloodbadge]] ×6 + [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] ×3 → [[Vampiric Timecharm]]|
|[[Celestial Timecharm]]|[[Celestial Timecharm]]|[[Wizardman Bureau]]|[[Shen Citrinitas]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Celestial Timecharm]] existed in the game under the ID of RIFT_TROPHY_MOUNTAIN before being obtainable.
  - It has also been leaked few days before its release, where it had a different name.

Scavenged Items are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items that have a 20% chance to be obtained from chests found using the [[Metal Detector]] in the [[Mines of Divan]] within the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Each item is given to their respective [[Keepers of Divan|Keeper]], and after all 4 have been given, the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]] can be obtained.
The chance to obtain Scavenged Items can be increased by up to 25% using a [[Mole Pet]], for a final chance of 45%.
|Icon|Item|Associated; Keeper|
|-|-|-|
|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Lapis Sword|Keeper of Lapis|
|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Golden Hammer|Keeper of Gold|
|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Diamond Axe|Keeper of Diamond|
|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Emerald Hammer|Keeper of Emerald|
- The [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Golden Hammer]] and [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Emerald Hammer]] can be combined in an [[Anvil]] in the [[Gold Mine]] to craft the [[Scavenged Iron Hammer]].
## Trivia
- Though not being soulbound, all tools have the properties as if they were co-op soulbound.

# Secret Bingo Card (Item)
Categories: Special items, Items, Events, Bingo
[Secret Bingo Card (Item)]
Type: [[Collectable Items]]
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Obtained during the [#]th Secret Bingo Event during <month> <year> for completing the entire Bingo Card!
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SECRET_BINGO_CARD
Tooltip: Obtained during the #th Secret Bingo Event during [month] [year] for completing the entire Bingo Card!; Player: [player]; Playtime: [playtime]; Placement: [placement]; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL
Secret Bingo Card is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Collectable Items|Collectable]] item. It is obtained by completing every [[Bingo|Goal]] on a [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]] [[Events|Event]]'s [[Bingo|Bingo Card]].
## Obtaining
### Bingo
A Secret Bingo Card can be claimed from [[Jerry]] after completing all 20 [[Bingo|Goals]] on a [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]] [[Events|Event]]'s [[Bingo|Bingo Card]].
## Usage
A Secret Bingo Card shows stats about which [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]] event's [[Bingo|Bingo Card]] was completed, which player completed them, and how long they took.
## Trivia
- The playtime indicated refers specifically to the time the player spent online on the bingo profile prior to completing the entire [[Bingo|Bingo Card]], not the time since the start of the Bingo event.
- Community goals do not affect obtaining the card, whether it be not having contributed in one or a goal not reaching its first tier.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 27, 2023|Added Secret Bingo Card.|
Linked from: [[Bingo (disambiguation)]], [[Collectable Items]]

# Secret Railroad Pass
Aliases: Railroad, Railroad Pass, Railroad pass
Categories: Special items, Items
[Secret Railroad Pass]
Type: Quest Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Mithril]] 8x [[Corleonite]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SECRET_RAILROAD_PASS
Tooltip: Quest Item; Show this pass to the Ticket Master.; * Soulbound * SPECIAL
The Secret Railroad Pass is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item used to unlock the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]].
## Obtaining
The Secret Railroad Pass can be created in [[The Forge]], after the player talks to [[Dulin]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Secret Railroad Pass]] 1 Secret Railroad Pass
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Ruby Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Ruby Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]]
  - 2 [[Refined Mithril]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 8 [[Corleonite]]
## Usage
During the quest Beyond the Wall, the player gives the Secret Railroad Pass to the [[Ticket Master]] or [[Station Master]] in order to unlock the [[Dwarven Base Camp]].
## History

A Shining Coin is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item used as a [[Quests|Quest Item]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Obtaining
A Shining Coin can be obtained as a [[Floor Drops|Floor Drop]] in the Haunted Biome in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Usage
### Gimmiegold Shard
A Shining Coin can be thrown in the basement of the mansion in the Haunted Biome to summon a [[Gimmiegold]], which eats the Shining Coin.

# Shrimp the Fish

[Item Properties]

Item ID: SHRIMP_THE_FISH
Tooltip: I achieved nothing.; SPECIAL
I achieved nothing.
Shrimp the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained from [[Ender Nodes]].
## Obtaining
Shrimp the Fish has a 0.095% or 0.175% chance to drop from [[Ender Nodes]] in [[The End]].
## Usage
Shrimp the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- Finding Shrimp the Fish will give the player the secret achievement [[Achievements|Shrimp!?!]].
  - Shrimp the Fish is referred to as "Shrimp the Traveler Fish" in the achievement.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DCTR_SPACE_HELM
Tooltip: A rare space helmet forged from shards of moon glass; To: Unknown From: Unknown; Edition #0; January 1970; This item can be reforged!; SPECIAL HELMET
The Space Helmet is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Helmets|Helmet]]. When worn, it cycles through the colors of the rainbow.
## Obtaining
### Spaceman
A Space Helmet is rewarded to the player after completing a trade with the [[Spaceman]] on [[The Garden]].
### Raffle of the Century
Space Helmet can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
The Space Helmet switches colors when worn.
## Trivia
- There used to be a bug with the [[Wardrobe]] that caused the helmet to disappear.
- One version not given by Dctr was spotted in 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] TorWolf's inventory, given by another 12[[Ranks|ADMIN]], 12Pensul.
- 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] LadyBleu gave out Space Helmets with her name on them for a short time. Editions #343 to #419 are all given by 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] LadyBleu.
- The players BenC1ark and TGWaffles have almost finished compiling a list of every Dctr's Space Helmet to exist, they have the names and locations of 413/419 Dctr's Space Helmets on their Google Sheet
- In most cases, Dctr executed the command /spacehelm [player] for a player after he received 750,000,000 [[Coins]] from them.
  - A few were obtained from Dctr's [[Auction House]].
## Gallery
Edition #1 as seen on the Auction House

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPECKLED_TEACUP
Tooltip: Quest Item; A garish ornament that is oddly beloved by Brynmor, owner of th Dwarven Tavern. If you return it to him, he will give you his Abiphone Contact.; * Soulbound * SPECIAL
The Speckled Teacup is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Quest Item.
## Obtaining
A Speckled Teacup can be obtained while [[Fishing]] in the [[Abandoned Quarry]]. It has a 10% (1 in 10 catches) chance to replace a [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]].
|Treasure Chance|Catch Chance|
|-|-|
|5 [[Treasure Chance]]|0.05% (1 in 2,000.0 catches)|
|15 [[Treasure Chance]]|0.15% (1 in 666.7 catches)|
|30 [[Treasure Chance]]|0.30% (1 in 333.3 catches)|
## Usage
A Speckled Teacup can be given to [[Brynmor]] in exchange for their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
## Trivia
- On initial release, the chance to catch a Speckled Teacup was approximately 0.00184% (1 in 54,300 catches) at base, or 0.00773% (1 in 12,929 catches) with the maximum treasure chance of 21% at the time.

# Storm the Fish
Aliases: Storm The Fish
Categories: Special items, Items, Catacombs Loot, The Fish
[Storm the Fish]
Type: Fish Family ([[The Fish Family]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Be glad you'll never have to face me!
Obtained: Floor VII Bedrock Chests
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STORM_THE_FISH
Tooltip: Be glad you'll never have to face me!; SPECIAL DUNGEON ITEM
Be glad you'll never have to face me!
Storm the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] (7) [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Reward Chests]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Storm the Fish has a chance to appear from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|6.4313% (1 in 15.5 runs)|6.0113% (1 in 16.6 runs)|61|10|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|3.4296% (1 in 29.2 runs)|5.989% (1 in 16.7 runs)|61|5|
## Usage
Storm the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- Due to having low quality and low weight values, Storm the Fish very rarely appears in the first slot of [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]], but much more frequently in later slots.
- It is one of three [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] items that are found in Catacombs [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] (7) [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Reward Chests]], along with [[Goldor the Fish]] and [[Maxor the Fish]].
- Storm the Fish is a reference to the then-removed Boss named [[Storm]].
  - In update 0.12.3, Storm ended up being one of the bosses players must face on Floor 7.
- Storm the Fish's quote is outdated because he is available to fight on Floor 7 and Master Mode Floor 7.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 23, 2026|Storm the Fish can now be sold to NPCs for 50,000 [[Coins]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|Added Storm the Fish.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Storm the Fish can now be sold to NPCs for 50,000 [[Coins]].|

# The Builder's Clay
Aliases: Dueces's Builder Clay
Categories: Special items, Armor, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Hypixel Gifts, Helmet
A block that was used to build the world around you.
[The Builder's Clay]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: A block that was used to build; the world around you.
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUECES_BUILDER_CLAY
Tooltip: A block that was used to build the world around you; To: Unknown From: Unknown; Edition #0; January 1970; This item can be reforged!; SPECIAL HELMET
The Builder's Clay is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] and [[Collectable Items|Collectable]] item given by 12 Dueces, a former 12[[Ranks|ADMIN]] of the [[Hypixel|Hypixel Network]]. When worn, it creates a ring of fire particles above the player's head.
The Builder's Clay has been spotted in many 12[[Ranks|ADMIN]]'s inventories, as well as given to a few players.
According to signs on 12Dueces' island, The Builder's Clay might be given as a reward for a building contest he might hold in the future.
## Obtaining
There were two ways for players to obtain the Builder's Clay:
- By 12 Dueces himself visiting the player's island. If he thought the island looked well built, he would give one for free.
- By visiting 12 Dueces's island and paying him 10 billion coins, just like how players obtain a [[Space Helmet|Dctr Space Helmet]].
## Gallery
- The Builder's Clay worn by Dueces
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 7, 2021|Dueces's Builder Clay was first spotted being worn by 12 Dueces|
|Unknown Date|Dueces's Builder Clay was renamed to The Builder's Clay.|

## Obtaining
The Cake can be obtained by continually speaking to the [[Royal Resident]] in the [[Barracks of Heroes]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] for about 20 minutes.
Once obtained, it can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,333,337 [[Coins]].
## Trivia
- The name and description is a reference to the phrase "The cake is a lie" from the Portal series.
- The buy price of this item is "1,333,337", while the sell price is "1337". The numbers may be a reference to "leetspeak", where numbers and symbols are substituted into words to make them look like letters.

## Obtaining
A Villager Doll is rewarded to the player once they contribute to the [[City Projects|Hub Revamp]] City Project at least 10 times. The player's name, number of contributions, along with the date it was obtained will be displayed in the item's lore.
## Trivia
- The Villager Doll's description likely refers to a practice known conjecturally as "hub jumping", where a player runs around the [[Hub Island]] and jumps between the roofs of buildings aimlessly.

- Wearing a [[Bonzo's Mask]] with [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration I]]
- Having Completed the [[Weird Sailor]] dialogue.
The player must have donated at least 1,000,000 [[Coins]] to the [[Kuudra Believer]] less than 30 minutes earlier to get the [[Weird Sailor]] to appear, as well as requires winning a [[Kuudra]] fight during the time to reach him.
This item can only be obtained once per profile.
## Usage
The player must have obtained a Wet Napkin, but does not need to physically have the item in their possession, to be able to obtain the Kuudra Follower Helmet. The Kuudra Follower Helmet is obtained by donating the result of the formula in the Wet Napkin's lore to the [[Kuudra Believer]] in [[Coins]].
## Bugs
- It is possible for a player to obtain the credit for fishing up a Wet Napkin without actually obtaining the item. This most commonly happens when it is replaced by a special treasure roll that takes priority, such as a [[Bouncy Beach Ball]], a [[Treasure Dye]], or a [[Wet Book]].
  - Despite the player not actually receiving the item, nor a chat message about it, they generally still get credited for it, meaning they cannot fish it up again, but they are able to continue the Kuudra Follower questline as if they had it.
  - There is no in-game way to check whether a player has gotten the credit, but it is visible on the Hypixel Public API.
    - Players can use the /hunt progress command with HuntBot in Official Hunters Discord to check this.
## Trivia
- There are 29 hidden Among Us characters scattered throughout the map art.
  - The number 29 corresponds to two obfuscated characters in the Wet Napkin's formula.
- Any [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration]] value over Ⅰ on the [[Bonzo's Mask]] does not work. Because of the fact that the [[Weird Sailor]] says he can hold his breath for 15 seconds.
- Despite being displayed as a [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]], it is a guaranteed drop.
- Whether the [[Weird Sailor]] is talked to or not doesn't affect the obtaining.
- The Wet Napkin doesn't need to be fished up before [[Weird Sailor]] disappears.
- It was believed that the Wet Napkin requires owning [[Herring the Fish]] to obtain, however it was then proved wrong.
## Gallery
- The Wet Napkin's map art.
## History

|Jul 27, 2022|Added Wet Napkin.|
|Oct 12, 2022|Wet Napkin was discovered.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|[[Ice Bait]] can no longer be used outside [[Jerry's Workshop]], making the Wet Napkin unobtainable.|
|Apr 3, 2026|Fixed Wet Napkin being unobtainable.|

Wholesale Wheat can be harvested by the wheat field in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. This field can be found by climbing up the ladder near the Cavern biome's entrance, and is located at [[Coordinates|-110, 81, 38]].

# Bat Firework
Categories: Spooky Festival, Rare items, Items, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Bat Firework is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Bat Firework]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Fear Mongerer]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Fear Mongerer]]
Buy: 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Green Candy]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BAT_FIREWORK
Tooltip: Launches a firework that explodes into several Bat Piñatas, each dropping a bunch of candies. Only usable in the Village.; Cooldown: 30s; Bat Piñata drops do not count towards leaderboard progress!; RARE
The Bat Firework is a [[Rarity|RARE]] Miscellaneous Item that launches a firework that explodes into several [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]], which drop [[Green Candy]]. It is only usable in the [[Village]].
Each Bat Firework spawns a random number of bats, ranging from eight to twelve bats. In order to launch it, you have to have it in your hand and hit the floor or a wall with it.
## Obtaining
A Bat Firework can be bought from the [[Fear Mongerer]] for 100 [[Green Candy]].
## Purple Candy
The player who launches a Bat Firework receives between 5 and 64 [[Purple Candy]].
|Drop|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|5 [[Purple Candy]]|40|34.483% (1 in 2.9)|
|10 [[Purple Candy]]|40|34.483% (1 in 2.9)|
|15 [[Purple Candy]]|20|17.241% (1 in 5.8)|
|20 [[Purple Candy]]|10|8.621% (1 in 11.6)|
|25 [[Purple Candy]]|5|4.310% (1 in 23.2)|
|64 (1 stack) [[Purple Candy]]|1|0.862% (1 in 116)|
## Tips
- After obtaining all items that cost [[Green Candy]], spending the excess into Bat Fireworks is a good idea to "convert" them into an average of 11 [[Purple Candy]] per Bat Firework.
- The 30 seconds cooldown can be circumvented by swapping lobbies via the [[Hub Selector]].
## Trivia
- Bat Fireworks used to directly put Candy directly into the player's inventory rather than drop them.
- The player who launches it must have a [[Trick or Treat Bag]], [[Spooky Sack]] or [[Events Sack]] in their inventory or [[Sack of Sacks]].
- Although Bat Fireworks can only be spawned in the [[Village]], the bats themselves can travel all around the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
- A player can only collect up to 25 [[Green Candy]] from each firework no matter who launches it.
- The candies collected from the [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]] do not count towards the score in the [[Spooky Festival]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed not being able to use a Bat Fireworks whilst having a [[Spooky Sack]] in your inventory.|
|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Fixed not being able to use a Bat Fireworks whilst having a [[Spooky Sack]] in your [[Sack of Sacks]].|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Fixed not being able to use a Bat Fireworks whilst having an [[Events Sack]] in your [[Sack of Sacks]].|

Additionally, it gives a +5% chance to get Candy from mobs during the event.|Bat Person Chestplate ([[Bat Person Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +30 All Combat Stats on this [[Armor]] piece are multiplied by 2x at night or 3x during the [[Spooky Festival]]! Additionally, it gives a +5% chance to get Candy from mobs during the event.|
[Total]
[Bat Person Armor Defense: +85]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Bat Powers Activate!; Upgrades your Grappling Hook and turns you into a true vigilante! Grants a +5% chance to find rare Candy.]
Bat Person Armor multiplies all Combat Stats by 2x at night: this is increased to 3x during the [[Spooky Festival]]. Additionally, each piece grants a +5% chance to obtain [[Green Candy]] and [[Purple Candy]] from mobs during the event.
Its Bat Powers Activate! full set bonus upgrades the [[Grappling Hook]] by removing its cooldown and increasing its velocity, alongside granting a +5% chance to find [[Purple Candy]].
Players wearing Bat Person Armor are immune to fall damage.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] Additionally, while damaging mobs, onomatopoeic indicators can be seen around the mob, reading Pow!, Wham!, Zap! or Bang![Confirm]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Vampire Minion I ([[Vampire Minion]])|Hemovibe I (1) ([[Hemovibe]])|1x Bat Person Helmet ([[Bat Person Armor]]) 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Hemovibe]]|Recipe: [[Hemovibe]] x10 ×8 + [[Bat Person Armor]] → [[Vampire Minion]]|

## Trivia
- Bat Person Armor is based off of Batman.
- The Bat Person Armor is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color with a hex color code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Obsidian Chestplate]], [[Squid Boots]], [[Tarantula Armor]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].
- Prior to Update 0.24.3, Bat Person Armor multiplied all stats at night or during the [[Spooky Festival]]. A common strategy would be to put a reforge granting a [[Wisdom]] stat, such as [[Reforging|Dimensional]] or [[Reforging|Sunny]], and use it to quickly level up skills.

# Bat Piñata
Aliases: Bat Pinata
Categories: Spooky Festival, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Bat Piñata is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Bat Piñata]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Spawn Location: [[Bat Firework]] [[Private Island]]
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Airborne]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Midnight Dye]] 0–1x [[Purple Candy]] 0–1x [[Bat the Fish]] 0–1x [[Green Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: BAT_PINATA_25
The Bat Piñata is a bat that only appears during the [[Spooky Festival]] and drops [[Green Candy]]. If spawned from a [[Bat Firework]], it gives 5–64 (1 stack) [[Purple Candy]] to the spawner. It also has a low chance of dropping a [[Bat the Fish]], although the ones spawned from [[Bat Firework]] are immune to damage and therefore cannot drop it.
While they can be spawned using the [[Bat Firework]], candy collected from these doesn't count towards your Candy Score.

|-|-|-|-|

|[[Purple Candy]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%|
|[[Bat the Fish]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[[Midnight Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/500k|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|

# Candy Corn
Categories: Spooky Festival, Uncommon items, Reforge stones, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Candy Corn is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Candy Corn]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Candied
Applied To: [[Armor]]
Apply Cost: 25,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 770 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CANDY_CORN
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Candied reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense; Candied Bonus Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
Candy Corn is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Candied]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], and increasing the chance for mobs to drop candy during the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Obtaining
Candy Corn has a 3.13% overall chance to drop from a [[Spooky Festival|Trick or Treat Chest]] during the [[Spooky Festival]].
While [[Foxy]]'s [[Foxy|A Time for Giving]] perk is active, [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] replace Trick or Treat Chests. Each Party Chest rolls its loot 2-4 times, with each roll having a 3.105% chance to contain Candy Corn.
## Usage
### Reforging
Candy Corn can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Candied]] reforge. This has no [[Mining]] requirement and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece.
Additionally, it grants the Candied Bonus, which makes mobs 1% more likely to drop candy during the [[Spooky Festival]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 HP ([[Health]]) +1 Def ([[Defense]])|+2 HP ([[Health]]) +1 Def ([[Defense]])|+4 HP ([[Health]]) +2 Def ([[Defense]])|+6 HP ([[Health]]) +3 Def ([[Defense]])|+8 HP ([[Health]]) +4 Def ([[Defense]])|+10 HP ([[Health]]) +5 Def ([[Defense]])|+12 HP ([[Health]]) +6 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|Candied Bonus; Mobs are 1% more likely to drop candy during the Spooky Festival.|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Applying the [[Reforging|Candied]] reforge to all four equipped armor pieces makes mobs a total of 4% more likely to drop candy during the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Trivia
- Candy Corn is based on candy corn, a real-world type of candy.

The Candy Ring is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that can be bought from the [[Fear Mongerer]]. It increases the chance of finding [[Green Candy]] and [[Purple Candy]] from mobs during the [[Spooky Festival]] by 10%. It is the upgraded form of the [[Candy Talisman]] and can be further upgraded into the [[Candy Artifact]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
It can be purchased from the [[Fear Mongerer]] during the [[Spooky Festival]] for 1 [[Candy Talisman]] and 64 [[Green Candy]].
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|Merchant|[[Fear Mongerer]] 1x [[Candy Talisman]] 64x [[Green Candy]]|
## Usage
Accessory:
Candy Ring
[Effects]
[Increases the chance for mobs to drop Candy by 10% during the Spooky Festival.]
### Shop Purchase
It can be used to purchase its upgraded form, the [[Candy Artifact]] from the [[Fear Mongerer]] for 1 Candy Ring and 32 [[Purple Candy]].
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|Merchant|[[Fear Mongerer]] [[Candy Ring]] 1x Candy Ring 32x [[Purple Candy]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2019|0.7.3|Added Candy Ring.|

### Crafting

- With a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Bat Pet]], this sword can deal up to 3 Damage to Spooky Mobs per hit.
- It is still recommended to have [[Enchantments]] on this sword, as a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]] can spawn from [[Trick or Treat Chest|Trick or Treat Chests]] in the middle of normal mobs, where [[Enchantments]] can help clear those mobs.

# Green Candy

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GREEN_CANDY
Tooltip: Candy can be used to trade with the Fear Mongerer during the Spooky Festival!; UNCOMMON
Green Candy is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Item that can be obtained mainly by killing mobs during the [[Spooky Festival]], among other sources.
## Obtaining
### Spooky Festival
During the [[Spooky Festival]], all [[Mob|Mobs]] have a 10% chance to drop Green Candy when killed. This chance can be increased with certain bonuses:
Increasing Green Candy Chance:
|Slot|Name|Extra Chance|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Helmet ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Chestplate ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Leggings ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|Spooky Boots ([[Spooky Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Helmet ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Chestplate ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Leggings ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|Bat Person Boots ([[Bat Person Armor]])|+5%|Grants a total of +20% chance with the full set equipped.|
|[[Candy Talisman]]|[[Candy Talisman]]|+5%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Ring]]|[[Candy Ring]]|+10%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Artifact]]|[[Candy Artifact]]|+15%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Relic]]|[[Candy Relic]]|+20%|Only the highest tier counts.|
|[[Candy Corn]]|Candied ([[Reforging]]) Reforge|+1%|Can be applied to all 4 [[Armor]] pieces to grant +4% total.|
|[[Bat Pet]]|[[Bat Pet]]|+0.1% → +20%|Chance increases with higher pet ([[Bat Pet]]) level.|
|Candy Scavenger I|Candy Scavenger ([[Doug]]) Perk|+2% → +10%|Chance increases with a higher perk level, up to level 5.|
||[[Foxy]] Sweet Benevolence perk|+30%||
With all of these upgrades, the base 10% candy chance increases to a 84% chance, or a 114% chance with [[Foxy]]'s perk. This chance is not increased by [[Magic Find]].

Helping kill a [[Headless Horseman]] can also grant Green Candy. Additionally, [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]] will drop Green Candy when flying around, which can be found randomly during the [[Spooky Festival]] or spawned from a [[Bat Firework]].
### Trick or Treat Chests
Opening a [[Trick or Treat Chest]] has a 26.04% chance to grant 4-8 Green Candy.
### Party Chests
Opening a [[Party Chest]] has a 25.88% chance to grant 6-12 Green Candy.
### Great Spook Tree
A [[Great Spook Tree]] can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] to occasionally generate Green Candy.
### Garden Visitors
Accepting the [[Fear Mongerer]]'s [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] request will grant a Green Candy or [[Purple Candy]].
## Usage
Green Candy can be sold on the [[Bazaar]] for varying amounts of coins or traded to the [[Fear Mongerer]] during the [[Spooky Festival]] for items.
Green candy awards the player +1 point. This is only the case for candies dropped directly from killing mobs or by collecting candies from Bats (excluding players' Bat Fireworks).

Categories: Spooky Festival, Epic items, Items, Events

The Horseman's Candle is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used to summon the [[Headless Horseman]] boss.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Horseman's Candle can be bought from the [[Fear Mongerer]] during the [[Spooky Festival]] for 32 [[Purple Candy]].
## Usage
Item:
Horseman's Candle
[Effects]
[Used to summon the Headless Horseman. This item can only be used at night!]
Only 1x [[Spooky Fragment]] can be obtained for damage ranking (1st, 2nd or 3rd). However, placing the candle guarantees 4 [[Spooky Fragment|Spooky Fragments]].
The [[Headless Horseman]] can only be spawned at night in the:
- [[Graveyard]]
- [[Wilderness]]
On the [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] gamemode, it can be spawned on the player's [[Private Island]].
It will announce who summoned it and where in the chat.
## Trivia
- The Horseman's Candle gives an average profit of for the summoner, or for a group of three.
  - It therefore costs to complete the [[Bestiary]] alone, or as a group.
